```coq
  - apply sty_rel_value; exact H.
  - apply sty_rel_value; exact H.
  - destruct H as [Hv _]; exact Hv.
  - destruct H as [Hv _]; exact Hv.
Qed.
```
Hmm, `simpl in H` may not unfold V if T is a variable... but in each branch T is a constructor, so V unfolds ✓. However, `induction T` then `simpl in H` where H : V E sigma (Ty_BVar n) t → simpl unfolds to sty_rel (nth_sem E n) t ✓.

```coq
Lemma C_lc : forall E sigma T t, C E sigma T t -> locally_closed_tm t.
Proof. intros; unfold C in *; tauto. Qed.

Lemma C_red : forall E sigma T t, C E sigma T t -> exists v, t -->* v /\ V E sigma T v.
Proof. intros; unfold C in *; tauto. Qed.

Lemma C_intro : forall E sigma T t v, locally_closed_tm t -> t -->* v -> V E sigma T v -> C E sigma T t.
Proof. intros; unfold C; auto. Qed.

Lemma C_value : forall E sigma T v, V E sigma T v -> C E sigma T v.
Proof.
  intros. unfold C. split. apply value_lc, V_value; auto.
  exists v. split; [apply multi_refl | auto].
Qed.

Lemma multi_trans : forall a b c, a -->* b -> b -->* c -> a -->* c.
Proof.
  intros a b c H. induction H; intros H2; auto.
  apply multi_step with y; auto.
Qed.

Lemma C_multi : forall E sigma T t t', t -->* t' -> C E sigma T t' -> locally_closed_tm t -> C E sigma T t.
Proof.
  intros E sigma T t t' Hm HC Hlc. destruct HC as [Hlc' [v [Hv HV]]].
  unfold C. split; auto. exists v. split; auto. eapply multi_trans; eauto.
Qed.

Lemma C_step : forall E sigma T t t', step t t' -> C E sigma T t' -> locally_closed_tm t -> C E sigma T t.
Proof. intros. eapply C_multi; [apply multi_step with t'; auto | auto | auto]. Qed.

Lemma V_arrow : forall E sigma T1 T2 t, V E sigma (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) t ->
  forall u, V E sigma T1 u -> C E sigma T2 (tm_app t u).
Proof. intros E sigma T1 T2 t [Hv H] u Hu. unfold C. auto. Qed.

Lemma V_all : forall E sigma T t, V E sigma (Ty_All T) t ->
  forall U Hlc R HR, C (mk_sem U Hlc R HR :: E) sigma T (tm_tapp t U).
Proof. intros E sigma T t [Hv H] U Hlc R HR. unfold C. auto. Qed.
```
Careful: V_arrow's `unfold C; auto` needs to show locally_closed_tm (tm_app t u) ∧ exists ...; H u Hu gives exactly the same conjunction, definitionally equal to C E sigma T2 (tm_app t u) ✓ after unfold C.

```coq
Lemma multi_app1 : forall t t' u, t -->* t' -> locally_closed_tm u -> tm_app t u -->* tm_app t' u.
Proof.

  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (tm_app y u); [apply ST_App1; auto | apply IHmulti; auto].
Qed.

Lemma multi_app2 : forall v t t', value v -> t -->* t' -> tm_app v t -->* tm_app v t'.
Proof.
  intros v t t' Hv H. induction H.
  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (tm_app v y); [apply ST_App2; auto | apply IHmulti].
Qed.

Lemma multi_tapp : forall t t' U, t -->* t' -> locally_closed_ty U -> tm_tapp t U -->* tm_tapp t' U.
```

multi_lc: `intros t t' H. induction H; intros Hlc; auto. inversion H; subst; auto.` The inversion of H (step) gives premises; but lc of y needs the step rules' premises. Let me prove multi_lc more carefully:

```coq
Lemma multi_lc : forall t t', t -->* t' -> locally_closed_tm t -> locally_closed_tm t'.
Proof.
  intros t t' H. induction H; intros Hlc; auto.
  - (* multi_step x y z : x --> y, y -->* z *)
    apply IHmulti. (* need lc y *)
    inversion H; subst; assumption.  (* H : x --> y; each step rule requires lc of the result? *)
Qed.
```
Let's check each step rule's conclusion lc: 
- ST_AppAbs: result open_tm t v; premises lc (tm_abs T t) and value v. Need lc (open_tm t v) — follows from lc of the abs (body lc at 1) and value v lc: open_tm_open_fwd ✓ (lc_tm_open_fwd with K=0, n=0).
- ST_App1: result tm_app t1' t2; premises t1 --> t1' and lc t2. Need lc t1' — not directly; but lc of the source tm_app t1 t2 gives lc t1, and by induction on step... Hmm, this is a step-preservation-of-lc lemma requiring induction on the step derivation. So multi_lc needs a separate `step_lc : step t t' -> locally_closed_tm t -> locally_closed_tm t'` proven by induction on step.

Let me prove step_lc:
```coq
Lemma step_lc : forall t t', step t t' -> locally_closed_tm t -> locally_closed_tm t'.
Proof.
  intros t t' H. induction H; intros Hlc; 
  try (inversion Hlc; subst; assumption).
```
Cases:
- ST_AppAbs: t = tm_app (tm_abs T t0) v, t' = open_tm t0 v. Hlc : lc (tm_app (tm_abs T t0) v) → lc (tm_abs T t0) and lc v. Lemma nth_sem_app_l : forall E E' i, i < length E -> nth_sem (E ++ E') i = nth_sem E i.

Recall the env_ok definition:
```coq
Definition env_ok (sigma : atom -> sem_ty) (rho : atom -> tm) (Gamma : context) : Prop :=
  forall x T, lookup_context x Gamma = Some T -> value (rho x) /\ V [] sigma T (rho x).
```

Fundamental theorem:
```coq
Lemma fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma rho,
    (forall x, locally_closed_tm (rho x)) ->
    env_ok sigma rho Gamma ->
    C [] sigma T (inst sigma rho t).
```

```coq
Fixpoint fv_tm (t : tm) : list atom :=

  | tm_bvar _ => []
```

I'll write the lemma with explicit induction patterns. Let me carefully lay out each case.

```coq
Lemma fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma rho,
    (forall x, locally_closed_tm (rho x)) ->
    env_ok sigma rho Gamma ->
    C [] sigma T (inst sigma rho t).
Proof.
  intros Delta Gamma t T Hder.
  induction Hder as
    [ Delta Gamma x T Hlook Hwf
    | L Delta Gamma T1 t2 T2 Hwf Hbody IHb
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 H1 IH1 H2 IH2
    | L Delta Gamma t0 T Hbody IHb
    | Delta Gamma t1 T U H1 IH1 Hwf
    | Delta Gamma t1 t2 T H1 IH1 H2 IH2 ].
```
